# Dota 6978: What Changed

The report was generated automatically on 2026-11-19. The draft covering all changes (parts: 647) was written by Claude Sonnet 5.5; the check against the source data and the final version were done by Claude Sonnet 5.5 (read-only access); the tables below were calculated without a model.

## Summary

### Main points

- **This is patch 7.42.** Data: `scripts/change_log.txt` received an entry `patch_name "7.42"`, `#DOTA_742_Update`; `steam.inf` shows build 6976 → 6978 (SourceRevision 11246070 → 11253575, 18 Nov 2026); a banner `front_page_742` appeared in the VPK. The changes are huge: 129 new modifiers, 54 new abilities and items, 18 new units, about 600 ability KV changes and about 270 unit changes, 440 new and 9286 changed schema classes.
- **A new cross-cutting mechanic: "Item Potency" (attack effectiveness) and buff "effectiveness".**
  - The property `MODIFIER_PROPERTY_ATTACK_POTENCY` was added.
  - `CDOTA_AttackRecord.m_flEffectiveness`, `CDOTA_Buff.m_flEffectiveness` and `CDOTA_BaseNPC.m_flBuffEffectivenessOverride` appeared in the schema.
  - The field `effectiveness = 45` was added to `dota_modifiers.proto`, and the flag `apply_effectiveness` was added to the ability value KV.
  - Illusions now read the new value `illusion_effectiveness`. Details are in section 2.
- **Glyph was reworked into a charge-based system.**
  - `CDOTAGameRules.m_iGlyphCharges[15]`, `m_flGlyphUseCooldowns` and `m_flGlyphActiveEndTimes` appeared, as well as `CDOTABaseGameMode.m_iCustomGlyphMaxCharges`.
  - A new method `SetCustomGlyphMaxCharges`, a new modifier `modifier_glyph_decrement`, HUD strings "Charges Available" and "Next Use In".
  - New items `item_horn_of_battle` and `item_horn_of_battle_greater`, a new event `dota_horn_of_battle_used`.
  - Tormentor now drops `item_horn_of_battle_greater`.
- **Neutral items were rebuilt.**
  - There are now 4 tiers instead of 5.
  - Instead of a flat list `items`, `scripts/npc/neutral_items.txt` now has `item_pools` with pools `pool_a`…`pool_d`.
  - Tier start times changed, `madstone_no_limit_time` 70:00 → 65:00, tier 4 `craft_cost` 10 → 14, and a `recraft_cost` 6 appeared.
  - 9 new neutral items were added. New neutral units: radigull with eggs, eimermole and its foreman, odobenus footman and frostmage, ogre seal with a cub, mud golem with the new Hurl Big Boulder, 6 "gold" variants with `neutral_gold_upgrade_ability`.
- **Hero and item reworks.**
  - New abilities: Chaos Knight (Armageddon), Undying (Bite), Lycan (Pack Hunter), Nyx (Scarab Metabolism), Dazzle (Through the Realm), Beastmaster (Summon Thunderhide), Weaver (Time Lapse Ally), Lone Druid (Summon Druid), Chen (Anoint).
  - Major reworks: Death Prophet, Pugna, Phantom Assassin Blur, Nature's Prophet Curse of the Forest, Meepo, Juggernaut, Morphling Replicate.
  - Removed from the shop: Revenant's Brooch, Angel's Demise, Helm of the Overlord and `item_desolator_2`. Added: Caduceus, Starforge, Skypiercer, Ristul's Devotion, Bloodslinger, Berserker's Sabatons and Aghanim's Blessing (`item_ultimate_scepter_2`, localized as "Aghanim's Blessing").
  - Item prices and cooldowns were changed en masse.
- **System changes.**
  - Creeps outside the lane are slowed (`modifier_path_in_lane` and `modifier_lane_creep_out_of_lane_slow`).
  - A "soft" unit collision mode appeared (`dota_unit_soft_collision`).
  - Roshan and Tormentor can "relocate" between pits (new chat lines).
  - In-game predictions were removed: panels, VPK files and the convar `dota_hud_ingame_predictions_hide`.
  - The base modifier class grew by 80 bytes, which caused field offsets to shift en masse.
  - WebRTC for voice was added to `libengine2`.

### New properties and methods: what they do

**`MODIFIER_PROPERTY_ATTACK_POTENCY` (413), `GetModifierPropertyAttackPotency`**
- **What it does.** The additive percentage of an attack's "Item Potency", that is, how fully item and on-hit effects trigger on that attack. 0 means 100%, a negative value means a reduction.
- **Evidence.**
  - Native modifier formulas from the dump:
    - `modifier_illusion` = `illusion_effectiveness − 100` (a constant −80 on the client);
    - `modifier_windrunner_focusfire` = `focusfire_attack_effectiveness − 100`;
    - `modifier_sand_king_scorpion_strike_attack_bonus` = `potency − 100`;
    - `modifier_weaver_geminate_attack_bonus` = `bonus_potency`;
    - `modifier_item_enhancement_evolved` = `bonus_potency`;
    - `modifier_juggernaut_blade_fury` = `scepter_attack_damage_pct`;
    - Sniper Headshot, Riki Backstab and Mirana Celestial Quiver are conditional formulas.
  - In `CDOTA_Modifier_Illusion::OnCreated` via `fdiff` a read of the new value `illusion_effectiveness` is visible, with a default of 0x41a00000 = 20.0. The function at address `0x5023260` returns this field minus 100. By default this is −80, which matches the client constant.
  - Localization: for Manta and Riftshadow Prism the illusions "have `illusion_effectiveness`% Item Potency". The descriptions of Mirana, Riki, Sniper, Weaver, Sand King, Pangolier, Void Spirit and Abaddon (Mist Coil scepter) use "Item Potency".
  - Schema: `CDOTA_AttackRecord.m_flEffectiveness` (float @124) and the bits `m_bNoLifesteal` and `m_bNoCrit`. New enum flags: `k_CreateAttackFlags_NoCrit=16` and `k_PerformAttackFlags_NoLifesteal=32`.
  - Next to it a KV value flag `apply_effectiveness: 1` appeared, for example on `bonus_chance` and `corruption_armor` of Stygian Desolator. It is parsed in libserver by the string `apply_effectiveness` in the value loader. What exactly is scaled is not directly visible.
- **How to use it in a custom game.** For Lua modifiers — you can't: `HasFunction` returned `false` (`modifier_properties.json`, in `enums.json` marked `broken`), the engine does not call `GetModifierPropertyAttackPotency`. It works only in native modifiers.
- **Reliability.** The meaning "item effectiveness percentage" — high. The exact place of application in the damage formula has not been traced.

**`MODIFIER_PROPERTY_HEALTH_DRAIN_CONSTANT` (414) and `…_PERCENTAGE` (415)**
- **What it does.** Constant health loss per second: a constant and a percentage.
- **Evidence.**
  - The only users in the dump are `modifier_item_armlet_unholy_strength` (`unholy_health_drain_per_second`) and `modifier_item_berserkers_sabatons_active` (a constant and `unholy_health_drain_pct_per_second`).
  - In the schema `CDOTA_BaseNPC` gained `m_flHealthDrainCounter` (float @4012).
  - For Armlet `Modifier_Armlet_UnholyStrength::OnIntervalThink` became shorter: 232 → 130 instructions, 35 → 17 calls. It looks like the manual HP loss tick was replaced by a property.
  - `CDOTA_Item_Armlet::OnToggle` and the new `CDOTA_Item_BerserkersSabatons::OnToggle` contain the strings of both items (`item_armlet`, `item_berserkers_sabatons`). This points to the toggles mutually switching each other off. A conclusion from strings, medium reliability.
- **How to use.** For Lua — you can't (`HasFunction=false`).
- **Reliability.** "HP loss per second" — medium. The percentage of maximum — a guess from the name.

**`MODIFIER_PROPERTY_HEALTHBAR_PIPS_FRAGILE` (416), `GetModifierHealthBarPipsFragile`**
- **What it does.** The native `modifier_fragile_pips` returns 1. Localization: "Fragile — Takes double damage from melee heroes and Roshan".
- **Context.** The health of a number of units is now counted in "pips":
  - Observer and Sentry Ward 100 → 8, Courier 6 → 8, Techies Land Mine 1 → 4, Juggernaut Healing Ward 1 → 4, Zeus cloud 16 → 32 (`StatusHealth`).
  - Zeus gained `tooltip_fragile_pips`: 8.
  - For Techies mine, radigull egg and stone of recall the already existing `HEALTHBAR_PIPS` is used.
- **How to use.** For Lua — you can't (`HasFunction=false`).
- **Reliability.** The "fragile pips" value is from localization; the double damage mechanic has not been traced in code.

**`MODIFIER_PROPERTY_HERO_KILL_BOUNTY_BONUS` (412), `GetModifierHeroKillBountyBonus`**
- **What it does.** By name — a bonus to the hero kill reward.
- **Evidence.** No native modifier in the dump uses it, `HasFunction=false`, `xref` for the name is empty. The description contains only the method name.
- **Reliability.** The meaning is unclear, this is a guess from the name. Useless for custom games.

**`CDOTA_BaseNPC:ReserveAbilitySlot(index)`, `IsAbilitySlotReserved(index)`, `RemoveAbilityFromIndex(index)`**
- **What they do.** Descriptions from the dump: a slot reservation is a slot without an ability that `AddAbility` will not fill (previously this was done with the ability `generic_hidden`). `IsAbilitySlotReserved` returns true if the slot is reserved. `RemoveAbilityFromIndex` clears the slot without removing the ability and removes the reservation.
- **Evidence.** In the schema `CDOTA_BaseNPC` gained the field `m_unReservedAbilitySlots` (uint64, @4920), probably a bitmask. The method strings are present in libserver. For `Ability4` of Juggernaut and in the slots of other heroes `generic_hidden` is still set, meaning the old way coexists with the new one.
- **Reliability.** The description is from the dump itself, high. The capacity of 64 slots is a conclusion from the field type.

**`CDOTABaseGameMode:SetCustomGlyphMaxCharges(maxCharges)`**
- **What it does.** Sets the maximum number of charges of the team Glyph (description from the dump). Next to it there were already `SetCustomGlyphCooldown` and `GetCustomGlyphCooldown`.
- **Evidence.**
  - A new field `m_iCustomGlyphMaxCharges` (int32) in `CDOTABaseGameMode` and `C_DOTABaseGameMode`; a network field registration function was added in libserver.
  - `m_iGoodGlyphCharges`, `m_iBadGlyphCharges`, `m_iGlyphCharges[15]`, `m_fGoodGlyphActiveEndTime`, `m_fBadGlyphActiveEndTime`, `m_flGlyphActiveEndTimes[15]`, `m_flGlyphUseCooldowns[15]` and `m_flGreaterHornOfBattleCooldowns[15]` were added to `CDOTAGameRules`.
  - Localization: "Charges Available: {charges_available}" and "Next Use In: {use_cooldown_time}".
  - Buildings now apply `modifier_glyph_decrement` with the parameter `decrease_amount` upon death (`CDOTA_BaseNPC_Building::vfunc#129`: the strings `modifier_glyph_decrement`, `decrease_amount` were added, `modifier_glyph_reset` and `tower1/tower2` were removed). The exact "decrement" mechanic has not been deciphered.
- **How to use.** Call it on `GameRules:GetGameModeEntity()`. A server-side method, the network syncs the value to the client.
- **Reliability.** Purpose — high. Interaction with the decrement — unclear.

**Event `dota_horn_of_battle_used`**
- **Fields.** One field `caster_userid` of type `EntityIndex` (from `events.json`).
- **What it means.** Use of the Greater Horn of Battle. Localization: "{caster_name} sounded the Greater Horn of Battle! Mega creeps are marching." `CHAT_MESSAGE_HORN_OF_BATTLE_USED = 128` was added to the protocol.
- **Items.**
  - `item_horn_of_battle` targets allied barracks across the whole map ("Must Target a Barracks", class `CDOTA_Item_Horn_Of_Battle`, `AbilityCastRange 99999`). It summons a wave of mega creeps (per the description — with a catapult and a standard bearer) on that lane.
  - `item_horn_of_battle_greater` — no target, waves on all lanes, `AbilityCooldown 30`, in code `OnSpellStart` reads `mega_waves` and looks up `npc_dota_spawner_*`.
  - Both are consumable, usable from the backpack. Tormentor drops the greater horn on death (`Miniboss::Spawn`, new line `item_horn_of_battle_greater`; localization "Whenever Tormentor dies it drops a Greater Horn of Battle").
- **Reliability.** High.

**`DOTA_UNIT_ORDER_INTERACT_WITH_TARGET` (43)**
- **Evidence.** A new value in `dotaunitorder_t` and in `.proto` (`DOTA_UNIT_ORDER_INTERACT_WITH_TARGET = 43`). The name is present in libserver. In the code next to it lie `GetInteractAbilityName` and `m_szInteractAbilityName` on Lantern and Watch Tower.
- **Reliability.** The purpose has not been traced in code. Most likely this is an "interact" order for objects with an interact ability, but this is a guess.

**`DOTA_ModifyGold_Midas` (23)**
- **Evidence.** A new gold change reason. In libclient `m_iMidasGold` appeared in `DataTeamPlayer_t` and `midas_gold`. The protocols `dota_match_metadata` (`midas = 10`) and `dota_scenariomessages` (`midas_gold = 160`) gained fields.
- **What this means.** Gold from Hand of Midas is counted in a separate statistic.
- **Reliability.** Medium.

**`DOTA_DEFAULT_UI_*` and `DOTA_HUD_VISIBILITY_*` = 32…35**
- **Values.** `PREGAME_HERO_SELECTION`, `INVENTORY_NEUTRAL_CRAFT`, `INVENTORY_NEUTRAL_SLOT`, `INVENTORY_TPSCROLL`. The enum size became 36.
- **What this means.** New HUD elements that can be hidden via `SetHUDVisible`. Clarification: only `DOTA_DEFAULT_UI_*` were added to Panorama. Use in code has not been traced, the conclusion is based on names.
- **Reliability.** Medium.

**Damage flags `DMG_*`**
- **What changed.** `DMG_BUCKSHOT` (2048), `DMG_BLAST_SURFACE` (4096) and `DMG_DISSOLVE` (8192) took the bits that previously belonged to `DMG_PREVENT_PHYSICS_FORCE`, `DMG_NEVERGIB` and `DMG_ALWAYSGIB`. `DMG_DROWNRECOVER` (131072) and `DMG_ACID` (262144) were added, `DMG_PARALYZE` and `DMG_NERVEGAS` were removed.
- **What this means.** This is a renumbering of inherited Source engine flags. Scripts that use the removed names will get `nil`.
- **Reliability.** The fact of the change — high. The gameplay role — unclear.

**New KV properties**
- **`PickupRadius`** (ability KV). Set on `item_tidehunter_fish` = 275.
- **`DirectKeybindAbilities`** (unit KV). A list of abilities with direct hotkeys: Kez (Falcon Rush, Talon Toss, Shodo Sai, Raven's Veil), Life Stealer (Consume) and others. At the same time the flag `DOTA_ABILITY_BEHAVIOR_DIRECT_KEYBIND` was removed from `AbilityBehavior` on the abilities of Invoker, Kez and Largo.
- **`MaterialGroup`** (unit KV). Example — `npc_dota_neutral_eimermole` = 1.
- **`show_occluded_outline`** (unit KV). Set on almost all units; it corresponds to the convar `r_dota_render_occluded_npcs` and the hint "render a silhouette version of the model" when a unit hides behind terrain, and in the schema `C_DOTA_BaseNPC.m_bShowOccludedOutline`.

**Panorama**
- **Attribute `languages`.** A list of localized variants of `src` (example `languages="russian,french"` for `foo.png` gives `foo_french.png`).
- **`fontsizesyncgroup` and `globalfontsizesyncgroup`.** The labels of a group share a common font size (reduced if necessary); the global variant works across layout files.
- **`renderscreenspaceparticles`.** An attribute without a description.
- **New panels.** `DOTABrewmasterScepterItemSlot`, `DOTABrewmasterScepterItems` (protocol `CDOTAClientMsg_BrewmasterAssignScepterItem{brewling, item_entindex}`), `DOTADarkCarnivalActSelector`, `DOTAHUDCursorAbilityInfo`, `DOTAUIItemPatchNotesHistory`, `SyncedFontSizeLabel`.
- **Reliability.** Based on descriptions and the VPK — high.

### For Custom Game Developers

**What may break existing addons**
- **Removed Lua and Panorama APIs.** `CDOTA_PlayerResource:GetEventGameUpgrades` and `GameUI.GetAbilityNameLocalizedForAbilityID`.
- **Removed panels.** `DOTABruiserSpellCard(Row)`, `DOTADeityBlessingPicker`, `DOTAIngamePrediction(Result/sContainer)`, `DOTAPredictionsButton`. `dota_hud_ingame_prediction*` and `dota_hud_predictions_button` were removed from the VPK.
- **Removed convars.** `dota_hud_ingame_predictions_hide` and `dota_claim_item_action_async_rewards_timeout_duration`. `fog_*skybox`, `fog_maxdensity` and `gl_clear` were also removed. The paired `net_limit_sv_message_process_time_ms_*_rate` were renamed to `…_rate_per_sec`.
- **Removed abilities, items and units.**
  - `meepo_fling`, `item_desolator_2` (the `desolator_2.png` panel was removed too), `item_recipe_revenants_brooch` (`item_revenants_brooch` itself is now a neutral) and the unit `npc_dota_unit_item_stone_of_recall`.
  - 17 modifiers, including `modifier_desolator_2_buff`, `modifier_item_revenants_brooch_active`, `modifier_item_solar_crest_armor_reduction`, `modifier_gungnir_debuff_mres`, `modifier_morphling_replicate_illusion`, `modifier_sven_warcry_aura` and `_passive`, `modifier_death_prophet_scepter`, `_attack_scepter`, `_spirit_siphon_buff` and `_fear`, `modifier_chen_zealot`, `modifier_dazzle_innate_weave_armor`, `modifier_lycan_wolf_bite_lifesteal`, `modifier_abaddon_borrowed_time_damage_redirect` and `modifier_abaddon_frostmourne_debuff`.
  - From `AbilityValues`, `shard_bonus_charges` (Spirit Siphon → `bonus_charges`) and `cooldown_reduction_pct` (Witchcraft) were removed.
- **Enum.** `ACT_RESET` was removed from Engine Enums (not noise). `DMG_PREVENT_PHYSICS_FORCE`, `DMG_NEVERGIB`, `DMG_ALWAYSGIB`, `DMG_PARALYZE`, `DMG_NERVEGAS` were removed.
- **Neutral items.**
  - If a custom game overrides `neutral_items.txt`, the format has changed: `items` → `item_pools`, tier 4 remains instead of tier 5, the tier 1 field `trinket_options` became 5, and `enhancement_options` did not change.
  - Enhancement variants: `MaxLevel` on `enhancement_*` was lowered (quickened, mystical, alert, brawny, tough 4 → 3; crude, keen_eyed, titanic 3 → 2; greedy 2 → 1; nimble 3 → 2), and on `enhancement_vital` it was raised 1 → 2.
  - `npc_dota_neutral_*` and tiers by `item_pools`: in the new format each pool has three items and `options 1`.
  - New pool items: Tier 1 — `red_ledger`; Tier 2 — `psychic_headband`, `safety_bubble`, `metamorphic_mandible`, `jidi_pollen_bag`, `brick`, `crippling_crossbow`, `imp_claw`, `gunpowder_gauntlets`, `defiant_shell`, `partisans_brand`, `spellslinger`, `mana_draught`; Tier 3 — `balmhook`, `vile_phial`, `revenants_brooch`; Tier 4 — `helm_of_the_hivemind` and others.
- **Lua modifiers.** Declaring the five new `MODIFIER_PROPERTY_*` (412–416) is pointless: the callbacks are not called (`HasFunction=false`).
- **Modifier size.** `CDOTA_Buff` grew from 6824 to 6904 bytes (+80, plus `m_flEffectiveness`@104), `CDOTA_BaseNPC` +144…152, `CDOTAGameRules` +560, `CDOTA_BaseNPC_Hero` +152, `CDOTA_PlayerResource` +592, `C_DOTA_Item` +8. This is the native layout; it does not affect Lua, but it breaks any binary extensions.
- **Ways of reading values.** Calls of `GetSpecialValueFor` inside the game received an additional parameter (in `GetAOERadius` Silence, `movss xmm0, [-1]` is visible before the call). The Lua method signature did not change.

**New in systems**
- **Off-lane creeps.** `modifier_path_in_lane` creates `modifier_lane_creep_out_of_lane_slow`. Its `OnIntervalThink` increases the stack counter by 5 per tick up to 100, and the slow = `−стаки / 4` %, i.e. at most −25% (formula in the dump: `stack × (−25) / 100`). The tick interval was not verified. Reliability is high for the mechanic and the formula.
- **Towers.** `modifier_tower_chaselimit_remover` and `modifier_tower_aura_chaselimit_remover` — assignment by name (lifting the chase restriction for towers), not decoded in the code.
- **XP fountain and experience.** `modifier_xp_fountain_defenders_gate` (states "stunned, invulnerable, not selectable, not on the minimap, no health bar") and `modifier_death_tick_xp` with `OnIntervalThink` of 1466 bytes. Purpose is inferred from names. New event `MODIFIER_EVENT_ON_BUILDING_KILLED` in `modifier_xp_fountain_aura`.
- **Soft collisions.** New convars:
  - `dota_unit_soft_collision` — per the hint in the code: ground units not controlled by a player stop blocking each other and are pushed apart by impulses;
  - `dota_unit_separation_padding` — the distance beyond the hull radii at which pushing apart begins;
  - `dota_unit_separation_max_speed` — the push-apart speed at full overlap;
  - `dota_unit_separation_acceleration` — the maximum change of the push-apart speed per second.
  Default values were not verified in the dump.
- **Other convars.**
  - Rendering and interface: `dota_outline_brightness`, `dota_outline_thickness`, `dota_bright_glows`, `dota_hud_custom_label_name_offset`, `dota_cache_clientside_wearables`.
  - Spawn group: `spawngroup_*`, `cl_spawngroup_unload_destroys_networked_entities`, `spawn_group_list_filters`. `SPAWN_GROUP_DONT_SAVE_RESTORE = 512` and `UM_EntitySpawnGroupChanged = 171` were added to the protocol.
  - Voice: `voice_webrtc_aec`, `_agc`, `_apm`, `_fixed_gain_db`, `_hpf`, `_ns`, `_ns_level`.
  - Navigation: `nav_gen_freeway_split_*`.
  - Profiler and network: `vprof_lite_*`, `net_decode_callback_queue_stats_*`.
  - Miscellaneous: `r_dota_water_reflect_combined`, `r_vulkan_present_wait`, `r_renderdoc_capture_frame_unscheduled`.
  - In total +73 −32; mostly debug-related.
- **Panorama.** A `Panorama { "PreprocessResources" "0" }` block appeared in `gameinfo.gi`. This is not an API.
- **Events and Lua.** The only new game event is `dota_horn_of_battle_used`. There are no new Panorama methods.

**New items** (KV `abilities.json`)
- **Purchasable.**
  - `item_caduceus` = Force Staff + Phylactery + recipe 600;
  - `item_starforge` = Meteor Hammer + Ultimate Orb + recipe 450;
  - `item_skypiercer` = Heaven's Halberd + Sange + Talisman of Evasion + recipe 300;
  - `item_ristuls_devotion` = Vladmir's Offering + Splintmail + Voodoo Mask, cost 3800;
  - `item_bloodslinger` = Sobi Mask + 2 Mantle + Blood Grenade + recipe 420;
  - `item_berserkers_sabatons` = Phase Boots + Armlet + recipe 2000 (cost 5950).
  The draft's assumption "Hood → Sabatons" is incorrect: Hood is a separate neutral item.
- **Neutral (`ItemIsNeutralActiveDrop 1`).** `item_stygian_desolator` (replaces `desolator_2`; `corruption_armor −13`, `corruption_duration 7`, with `apply_effectiveness`), `item_helm_of_the_hivemind`, `item_claszian_timepiece`, `item_brick`, `item_balmhook`, `item_red_ledger`, `item_berserkers_hood`, `item_vile_phial`, `item_nothl_sigil`.
- **Consumable.** `item_stone_of_recall` (placed with a 60 s cooldown; allied heroes across the entire map can channel a teleport to the stone; in the text "immune to spells", "pip health").
- **Starting shop changes.** `item_ultimate_scepter_2` was added; `item_blitz_knuckles` and `item_quarterstaff` swap places (`quarterstaff` stopped being obsolete, `javelin` became obsolete).

**KV balance changes (selective, visible fields only)**
- **Items.**
  - Heaven's Halberd: cost 3300 → 2400, range 750 → 500, mana 25 → 50.
  - Bloodthorn: recipe 600 → 1350, cost 6400 → 6250.
  - Butterfly 5450 → 5000, Sange / Yasha / Kaya 2100 → 2000, Aether Lens 2275 → 2100, Kaya and Sange / Sange and Yasha / Yasha and Kaya 4200 → 4000.
  - Maelstrom: recipe 0 → 900. Orb of Corrosion 1050 → 880, Pipe cooldown 60 → 50, Smoke of Deceit 3 → 4 charges per 330 s.
  - Neutrals: Fallen Sky cooldown 25 → 18, Heavy Blade 30 → 24, Seeds of Serenity 35 → 28, Imp Claw 5 → 7, Iron Talon obsolete.
- **Heroes (base).**
  - `npc_dota_hero_base` StatusHealth 120 → 200.
  - Death Prophet: range 600 → 500, damage 22–32 → 18–28, speed 290 → 295.
  - Pugna: range 630 → 600, agility 21 → 16, intelligence 27 → 29.
  - Meepo: primary attribute Agility → All, damage 29–35 → 20–26.
  - Lion: agility 15 → 18. Bloodseeker: speed 285 → 290.
- **Abilities.**
  - Techies Land Mines: charges removed, cooldown 0 → 0.25, mana 110/140/170 → 125/175/225.
  - Medusa Gorgon Grasp: damage physical → magical.
  - Kez Raptor Dance: damage pure → physical.
  - Ancient Apparition Ice Blast: 250/325/400 → 200/300/400.
  - Ursa Earthshock: 75/125/175/225 → 80/140/200/260.
- **Neutral creeps.** Reward budgets were reduced by 3–10%. `npc_dota_neutral_kobold` received increased damage, HP and reward, and forest trolls received new ability slots.

### Game Code Changes

Scale: libserver — 55,331 changed, 6,001 added and 6,310 removed functions; libclient — 56,693, 5,529 and 6,874. The overwhelming majority of changed functions are recompilation mechanics with no change in logic:
- modifier field shift by +0x50 (`0x1aa8` → `0x1af8`);
- `CDOTA_BaseNPC` field shift (`0xcd4` → `0xd3c` and others);
- vtable slot shift by +2…+3 (396 → 398 and others);
- a new call to slot 62 (`+0x1f0`) before reading a special value and an added constant `−1` in `GetAOERadius`/`GetSpecialValueFor` — recompilation for the new value-reading signature;
- differences in `+0x410/−0x400` constants in the service `vfunc#7/#8` of modifiers.

These are not separate "hero changes"; such functions are grouped together. The full list is in `reports/6978-functions.md` and `so-query.py funcs`. Below are only the substantive changes, verified via `fdiff` and `func`.

**Glyph, Tormentor and the base**
- `CDOTA_Unit_Miniboss::Spawn` (0x4781c60, 896 → 1402 bytes): the string `item_horn_of_battle_greater` was added. A new `CDOTA_Unit_Miniboss::vfunc#531` (6475 bytes) was added with the strings of both horns, `item_aghanims_shard`, `modifier_item_aghanims_shard`, `tormentor`, `duration` — the logic of death rewards. Localization confirms: the big horn always drops; Aghanim's Shard — from the second kill (if the entire team already has a Shard, the reward is different).
- `CDOTA_Item_Horn_Of_Battle::GetCustomCastErrorTarget` and `CDOTA_Item_Horn_Of_Battle_Greater::OnSpellStart` were added (see section 2).
- `CDOTA_Modifier_Fountain_Glyph::OnCreated` (0x5118ac0): reads of `duration` and `next_charge_time` were added, the code shrank from 128 → 107 instructions. The functions in `CDOTAGameRules` (0x518b2a0, 0x518cc10), which previously applied `modifier_fountain_glyph` and sent `dota_glyph_used`, were rebuilt: they now work with charges and write `glyph_used_good/bad` events.
- Reliability: high for the fact itself and the presence of charges; the detailed cooldown formulas were not decoded.

**Illusions**
- `CDOTA_Modifier_Illusion::OnCreated/OnRefresh`: a read of `illusion_effectiveness` (default 20) was added, which is returned as `ATTACK_POTENCY = value − 100`. The result is confirmed by `func` at 0x5023260 (subtraction of the constant 100) and by the client constant −80.
- Reliability: high.

**Death Prophet** (verified by `funcs`/`fdiff`, consistent with KV and localization)
- From `Silence`, `Carrion Swarm` and `Spirit Siphon::OnSpellStart`, the strings `death_prophet_exorcism`, `modifier_death_prophet_scepter`, `spirit_duration`, `target` and the constant 20 were removed: the old link "the scepter empowers Exorcism on cast" was removed. `death_prophet_exorcism` lost its `HasScepterUpgrade` in KV, cooldown 150 → 145/135/125.
- Spirit Siphon (KV): `HasShardUpgrade` 1 → None, `HasScepterUpgrade` None → 1, `SpellImmunityType` NO → YES. In `OnCreated/OnRefresh`, `damage_pct`, `movement_steal`, `shard_fear_duration`, `shard_consecutive_siphon_duration` disappeared, and `max_health_damage_pct`, `attack_slow`, `movement_slow` were added. In `OnIntervalThink`, the application of `modifier_death_prophet_spirit_siphon_fear` was removed. Scepter localization: "steals attack_slow attack speed … drains an additional max_health_damage_pct% of max health". `SpiritSiphon_Slow::OnCreated`: instead of the growing `max_moveslow`/`max_moveslow_time` — fixed `attack_slow` and `movement_slow`. Fear (`Siphon_fear`) moved to `modifier_death_prophet_crypt_swarm_fear` (Carrion Swarm received `HasShardUpgrade`, flag `apply_debuff`).
- Witchcraft: `cooldown_reduction_pct` was removed, `spirits` and `attack_damage_pct` were added; the new modifier `Witchcraft_Spirit` calls the common spawn `dota_death_prophet_exorcism_spirit` (now via `CEntitySpawner` with the strings `Spawn`/`CALLBACKS`). Exorcism: `movement_bonus` was replaced by the flag `unslowable`.
- Reliability: high for the composition of changes; "fear moved" is a conclusion based on the set of strings and the API dump.

**Pugna**
- Life Drain: a flag read was added in `GetBehavior`; `OnSpellStart` was rewritten; `drain_id` and `life_drain` appeared in `Modifier_Pugna_LifeDrain::OnCreated` (instead of `health_drain` and `ally_healing`); `OnIntervalThink` lost `bonus_heal_pct`. New `modifier_pugna_life_drain_caster` and `GetCustomCastErrorTarget` with `#dota_hud_error_cant_cast_twice` (cannot be applied twice). KV: channel 10 → `AbilityDuration 10`, `HasScepterUpgrade` 1 → None, mana 115/160/205 → 100/150/200. The channel behavior is now "duration per target" — a conclusion based on KV and the new modifier type, reliability medium.
- Oblivion Savant: KV received `HasScepterUpgrade`, `modifier_pugna_oblivion_savant_recast` was added (`OnIntervalThink` 680 → 334 bytes, 108 → 101 instructions); `tower_scale` in `OnCreated` 1 → 0.1 — the meaning (default value?) is unclear.
- Decrepify: mana 80 → 50/60/70/80; `npc_dota_pugna_nether_ward` and `npc_dota_unit_tombstone1…4` were added to the target check.

**Phantom Assassin**
- Blur is now an active ability: KV — `AbilityCastRange 600`, `AbilityCastPoint 0.3`, mana 40, `duration 20`, `radius 600`, `break_on_attack 1`, `fade_duration 0.8`, `immediate_cast` and `purge_on_cast` are set only by the Shard. Localization: "impossible to see until she comes near enemy heroes or towers".
- `BlurActive::OnIntervalThink`: `modifier_phantom_assassin_blur_revealed` was added. According to the dump, invisibility and truesight immunity are in effect while this modifier is absent. `modifier_phantom_assassin_blurred` were also created. Reliability is high; the "visibility" radius per KV is 600.

**Other heroes (confirmed by dump modifiers and KV)**
- Lone Druid: `summon_druid_death_deferral` holds `MIN_HEALTH` and prevents death while the Spirit Bear is alive nearby (`attack_leash_range`); `summon_druid_downed` = invulnerable, invisible, not selectable, uncontrollable while the druid is "down"; messages "Lone Druid Is Down", "Cannot Resummon Right Now".
- Chaos Knight: Armageddon gains speed for moving without stopping (`MOVESPEED_BONUS_CONSTANT = стаки`), with a living phantasm a charge can be activated (`armageddon_charge`, `formation`, `windup`); Armageddon's Flank is a separate ability; error "Requires A Living Phantasm".
- Undying Bite: `modifier_undying_bite` (+attack range, cannot miss), `bite_zombie` turns the hero into a mute melee zombie for `zombie_duration`.
- Nyx: Scarab Metabolism replaced Neuro Sting in the slot; Beastmaster: Thunderhide instead of Drums of Slom; Weaver: Time Lapse Ally shares its cooldown with Time Lapse (`AbilitySharedCooldown`).
- Furion: Curse of the Forest became passive (`BEHAVIOR_PASSIVE`, cooldown and mana removed), separate modifiers `curse_of_the_forest_instance/_passive`.
- Meepo: Megameepo is no longer granted by the scepter (`IsGrantedByScepter 1 → 0`), cooldown 90 → 120; Petrify is now granted by the scepter; Divided We Stand received a Shard variant. "`meepo_fling_release` is bound to `divided_we_stand`" from the draft is not confirmed — in the unit, `meepo_fling_release` simply took slot 7.
- Juggernaut: `swift_slash` is no longer granted by the scepter; Blade Fury with the scepter allows attacking while moving (`ATTACK_WHILE_MOVING_TARGET`, `ATTACK_POTENCY = scepter_attack_damage_pct`), without the scepter it remains Disarmed.
- Sniper Headshot: now uses `ATTACK_POTENCY` (`attack_effectiveness`) and a new `modifier_sniper_headshot_knockback`.
- Techies: mines and `modifier_techies_mad_pips` now have invulnerability to all damage types and health "pips" (`ABSOLUTE_NO_DAMAGE_*`, `HEALTHBAR_PIPS`).

**Other systems**
- Armlet / Sabatons: see "HEALTH_DRAIN"; `item_armlet` is now `IGNORE_INVISIBLE`.
- Stone of Recall: `CDOTA_Item_StoneOfRecall::OnSpellStart` creates `npc_dota_stone_of_recall_summoned` with `modifier_kill`, `modifier_invulnerable` and `modifier_stone_of_recall_planted_hp`; `Stone_Of_Recall_Teleport::OnChannelFinish` places `modifier_item_stone_of_recall_teleport_timeout` (`teleport_use_cooldown`); casting is forbidden during `modifier_tusk_snowball_movement`. Reliability is high.
- Roshan / Tormentor: the scheme received `ERoshanSpawnPhase::ROSHAN_SPAWN_PHASE_AEGIS_HELD=3`; new chat lines about "relocating" to another pit/chasm.
- "Fuse" modifiers for neutrals: `modifier_do_not_cast_eggstremism/_purge/_seed_shot`, `modifier_neutral_cast_attempt_cooldown` — by names, protection against re-applying the AI ability; the code was not analyzed.

### Schema, Protocols, and Game Files

**SchemaSystem (classes +440, −71, ~9286; enums +9, −4, ~73)**
- Most of the "changed" entries are a change of the `uint32 → ?` type in dozens of unrelated classes (`m_id`, `m_nFlags`, `m_unLastHeroPickOrder`…). This looks like an artifact of type resolution in the dumper rather than a game change (low confidence in this conclusion).
- New game-related classes: abilities and modifiers of the new heroes and neutrals (see the list in section 3), `CDOTA_FoWRevealedRegion`, `CDOTA_Item_Balmhook`, `CDOTA_Item_BerserkersSabatons`, `CDOTA_Item_Brick`, `CDOTA_Item_HelmOfTheHivemind`, `CDOTA_Item_Recipe_Starforge`, `CDOTA_Item_Recipe_BerserkersSabatons`, `CDOTA_Ability_Neutral_Gold_Upgrade_Ability`, `CDOTA_Ability_MudGolem_HurlBigBoulder`.
- Offset shifts (one line per class, the cause is a new field or a base shift):
  - `CDOTA_Buff`: new field `m_flEffectiveness`@104, size +80, then `m_hScriptScope` shifts by +80; all modifier descendants +80.
  - `CDOTA_BaseNPC`: new `m_bCountsAsCreepForLifesteal`@3474, `m_flHealthDrainCounter`@4012, `m_unReservedAbilitySlots`@4920, `m_bHasConsideredNPCController`@6852, `m_flBuffEffectivenessOverride`@7432; size +152.
  - `C_DOTA_BaseNPC`: new `m_flSelectionHitboxScale`, `m_bShowOccludedOutline`, `m_flBuffEffectivenessOverride`; size +144.
  - `CDOTAGameRules`: Glyph and Horn fields (see section 2), `m_vecFoWRevealedRegions`, `m_arrArtifactPools`; `m_aNeutralChoicesLoaded` bool[5] → bool[4]; size +560.
  - `CDOTABaseGameMode`: `m_iCustomGlyphMaxCharges`, size +8.
  - `CDOTA_AttackRecord`: `m_flEffectiveness` and the bits `m_bNoLifesteal`, `m_bNoCrit`.
  - `C_DOTABaseAbility`: `m_flLastDoubleTapCastTime`@2084 (by name — a cast double tap).
  - `CAnimationLayer` +12 (`m_bDisableSequenceFadeOut`); Pulse classes received lambda values (`CPulse_LambdaDef`, `PVAL_LAMBDA`).
- Enums: `ECreateAttackFlags +NoCrit`, `EPerformAttackFlags +NoLifesteal`, `ERoshanSpawnPhase +AEGIS_HELD`, `EDOTA_ModifyGold_Reason +Midas`, `DOTA_CHAT_MESSAGE +HORN_OF_BATTLE_USED`, `EDotaClientMessages +BrewmasterAssignScepterItem`, `dotaunitorder_t +INTERACT_WITH_TARGET`, `modifierfunction +412…416` (`MODIFIER_FUNCTION_LAST` 412 → 417), `DOTAHUDVisibility_t` and `DotaDefaultUIElement_t` 32 → 36, `MovementCapability_t` (+`eTurnToStart`, `eAim`, `eForwardStopOnly`, −`eForwardStartOnly`), `InputBitMask_t −IN_ALL`.

**`.proto`**
- `dota_modifiers.proto`: `optional float effectiveness = 45`.
- `dota_clientmessages.proto`: `DOTA_CM_BrewmasterAssignScepterItem = 835` and `CDOTAClientMsg_BrewmasterAssignScepterItem {brewling, item_entindex}`.
- `dota_usermessages.proto`: `CHAT_MESSAGE_HORN_OF_BATTLE_USED = 128`.
- `usermessages.proto`: `UM_EntitySpawnGroupChanged = 171`; `GAME_PARTICLE_MANAGER_EVENT_SET_CONTROL_POINT_CLIENT_SNAPSHOT = 42`; new field `reuse_existing_sims` for physics particles.
- `gameevents.proto`: `GE_MoveWorldDecalsEvent = 215` (`mins/maxs/offset`).
- GC and event messages: `CMsgDeleteItem` / `k_EMsgGCDeleteItem = 1040`, `CMsgGCUseItemResponse`, `CMsgGCGiftedItems`, `custom_game_difficulty_level` (field 24 in the lobby parameters and 79 in the match), `CSODOTAPartyMember.EventGameLevel`, `CMsgEventActionGrantLootListRewardData`, expanded response codes of Dev messages (rename `DevDeleteEventActions` → `DevResetEventActions`); `CMsgDevGrantEventAction*`, `CMsgDevDeleteEventActions*` and other service event messages were removed.

- `network_connection.proto`: `NETWORK_DISCONNECT_STEAM_GROUPMEMBERSHIP = 86`.
- `source2_steam_stats.proto`: processor statistics (`cpu_num_logical`, `cpu_num_performance`, `cpu_num_efficiency`, `thread_pool_workers`).

**VPK Text Files and items_game**
- 150 files changed: `scripts/npc/heroes/*.txt` (almost all heroes), `items.txt`, `neutral_items.txt`, `npc_abilities.txt`, `npc_ability_ids.txt`, `npc_units.txt`, `portraits*.txt`, `shops.txt`, `bot_builds.txt`, 7 sections of `items_game/08-items-*.txt`, `change_log.txt`, `chat_wheel.txt` (last ID 832 → 846), `permanent_grants.eventdef`.
- 2 tournament template files were removed (`total_debuff_duration_with_medallion_solar_crest_as_hero`). In `core/scripts/mapbuilder_settings.txt` the flag `-steamaudio_gpu` was added, in `sound_operator_stacks.txt` the test block was removed.
- `shops.txt`: Revenant's Brooch, Angel's Demise, Helm of the Overlord, Soul Booster (moved), Javelin, etc. were removed from the shop; Bloodslinger, Caduceus, Starforge, Skypiercer, Ristul's Devotion, Sabatons, Aghanim's Blessing were added.

**VPK Index**
- dota: +678, −34, ~958; core: +19, −3, ~68.
- Added by folder:
  - `particles/units/` — 186 files: Enchantress (about 35), Chaos Knight (about 25), Lone Druid (about 20), Lycan (about 19), Omniknight (16), Underlord, Broodmother, Juggernaut, Kunkka, Dazzle, Gyrocopter, Undying, Leshrac, and others. For Enchantress there are no new abilities in the API; only KV values change and the "Spellbound" facet is removed, so the reason for the particles is unclear.
  - `particles/items5_fx/` — 93, `particles/neutral_fx/` — 67, `materials/models/` — 93, `panorama/images/` — 66.
  - `models/creeps/` — 29: `diregull`, `ogreseal`, `eimermole`, `n_creep_thunder_lizard_aghs`, `omniknight_golem`; `models/heroes/omniknight/golem`, `models/gameplay/stone_of_recall.vmdl`.
  - `particles/items_fx/` — 16 (Caduceus, Roshan's banner, new Glyph effects — "glyph_*_red", `glyph_tube_*`), `items2_fx/` — Blood Grenade (11), `items4_fx/` — Brick, `items8_fx/` — Poison Coating, `particles/ui/hud/pugna_oblivion_savant_*`, `particles/status_fx/chaos_knight_armageddon_illusion_status_effect.vpcf_c`.
  - `panorama/layout|styles`: `front_page_742`, `dota_ui_item_patch_notes_history`, `ui_dark_carnival_act_selector`, `dota_hud_brewmaster_scepter_items`, `dota_hud_cursor_ability_info`.

- New interface strings: the Dark Carnival event (Act 2 "Midway Madness", Oracle, Candyworks, Secret Rooms Pack II) — not related to the custom games API.

### Binaries

- **libserver.so.** 126.6 → 129.7 MB, code +1.28 MB, strings +2048/−506. About 6,000 new functions are classes of new abilities, modifiers, and items (RTTI +481/−98), the Glyph and Horn systems, Stone of Recall, Armlet/Sabatons. New convars: `dota_unit_*`, `nav_gen_*freeway*`, `snd_opvar_set_*`.
- **libclient.so.** New RTTI (+528/−147), 212 new game entity names; most of the changes are client copies of server classes and UI.
- **libengine2.so.** 9.5 → 9.9 MB, code +0.5 MB, exports +18/−169. Added exports `webrtc::ParseTypedParameter<…>` (WebRTC voice processing, convar `voice_webrtc_*`), new `cl_packetentities_merge_before_decode`, `cl_tickpacket_recvmargin_extra_async_usercmd_send_disabled`, `r_wait_for_shader_compiles`; the field `uncompressed_size` was removed from the network.
- **libanimationsystem.so, libparticles.so, libpulse_system.so, libpanorama.so, libscenesystem.so, librendersystemvulkan.so, libsoundsystem.so, libvphysics2.so.** Major internal updates (Pulse lambdas, NM sound events, Vulkan, Steam Audio); not related to the game API for custom games. libpanorama received about 60 other strings and 95 identifiers (including support for new attributes).
- **What is visible behind the API changes.** All new Lua and enum names are present in libserver/libclient as strings. For the 5 new modifier properties `xref`, the string is empty — the names are in a data table rather than in executable code; therefore their point of application cannot be found by a direct search. Dumper noise (`dump-noise.md`: shifts of the values of `ACT_DOTA_*`, appearance of `ACT_DOTA_CAST5_STATUE`, disappearance of `ACT_DOTA_TORRENT`) is not included in the report.

<details><summary>What was corrected in the draft during review</summary>

Checked selectively: I read parts 01 and 07, fragments 406 and adjacent ones, and cross-checked them against `api-changes.txt`, `chunk-01.txt`, KV, localization, and `so-query`. The main body of mechanical parts (about 500 of 647 summaries on `libserver`/`libclient`/engine) was not proofread.

- **Incorrect or unconfirmed.**
  - "`item_berserkers_hood` and `item_berserkers_sabatons` form an upgrade chain" (part 07): incorrect. Sabatons is a purchasable recipe of Phase Boots + Armlet + 2000, Hood is an independent neutral item.
  - "`meepo_fling_release` is now tied to `meepo_divided_we_stand`": the data only shows that the hero's slot 7 was taken by `meepo_fling_release`, and `meepo_fling` was removed. The link is not confirmed.
  - "Death Prophet's Spirit Siphon was removed or replaced" (part 01): incorrect. The ability remains, the fear was removed and moved to Crypt Swarm, the parameters were reworked, and the upgrade moved from Shard to Scepter.
  - "Possibly a slot reservation mechanism" and "possibly a Glyph setting": the guess status was removed — there are descriptions in the dump, schema fields, and Glyph charges in `CDOTAGameRules`.
  - "Possibly Spirit Siphon / Blur are 'revealed' — the meaning is unclear": clarified using KV and localization.
  - "Slot 62 and the constant `-1` — game logic does not change": supplemented — this is a new signature for reading values (related to buff effectiveness); by itself it does not change game effects.
  - "Panorama Enums — the same 11 values as in Lua": clarified that Panorama does not have `DOTA_HUD_VISIBILITY_*`, only `DOTA_DEFAULT_UI_*`, `INTERACT_WITH_TARGET`, `Midas` and 5 properties.
- **Missed in the draft (added).**
  - `HasFunction=false` for all five new properties.
  - The real meaning of `ATTACK_POTENCY` (Item Potency, illusions, `m_flEffectiveness`, `apply_effectiveness`).
  - Reading of `illusion_effectiveness`.
  - The meaning of `HEALTH_DRAIN_*` (Armlet and Sabatons) and `HEALTHBAR_PIPS_FRAGILE` (the "Fragile" description).
  - The Glyph and Tormentor reward rework, fields `CDOTAGameRules`.
  - Soft collision convars and their hints.
  - The neutral item format (`item_pools`, 4 tiers, times).
  - Neutral `item_stygian_desolator` as a replacement for `desolator_2`.
  - The composition of the new items with recipes and prices.
  - The property `DirectKeybindAbilities` instead of the flag `DIRECT_KEYBIND`.
  - The modifier `lane_creep_out_of_lane_slow` with a formula down to −25%.
  - The code of Pugna Life Drain and Phantom Assassin Blur.
  - The protocol `effectiveness`.
  - A summary of schemas by class and enum.
  - Patch 7.42 per `change_log.txt`.
- **Noise.** Shifts of `ACT_DOTA_*`, `ACT_DOTA_LOADOUT_STATUE`, and `ACT_DOTA_CAST5_STATUE` from Lua Enums were correctly excluded. `ACT_RESET` (Engine Enums) is not noise and is considered a real removal.

</details>
